expand_german_borders_aoi_category = {

    change_the_management_of_luxemburg_decision_ger = {
	    icon = generic_form_nation
        visible = {
			8 = {
				owner = {
			    	NOT = {
					    tag = GER
					}
		       	}
            }
        }
        available = {
			8 = {
				is_fully_controlled_by = GER
				OR = {
				   	resistance < 15
				   	compliance > 40
					any_country_with_core = {
						exists = no
					}
				}
            }
        }
		custom_cost_text = command_power_25
        custom_cost_trigger = {
            command_power > 24.9
        }
        complete_effect = {
            hidden_effect = {
                add_command_power = -25
            }
            GER = {
                set_state_owner = 8
            }
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
	}
